This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am operating as an independent scientific reviewer for the "simgate-modular-routing" project (EMNLP 2026 via ARR, DDL May 25, 2026). My role is to monitor and review experiment results as workers complete them, verify data integrity, check GPU compliance, and post findings to Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`. All output must be in Chinese. This session continued from a previous context window. The primary task was monitoring the reflexion-dose-response-n345 experiment (N=3,4,5 reflexion on westd-18266) through its final completion, while conducting independent data verification and identifying issues for the Director.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Death Rate (DR)**: Core metric — `is_death = (score <= -100)`. Multiple definitions: "final death rate" (last trial is death / total episodes), "per-trial conditional DR" (death at trial k / episodes reaching trial k)
   - **Dose-response curves**: Retry (N=1..5) and Reflexion (N=1..5) showing how death rate changes with more trials
   - **U-shape per-trial trajectory**: In N=4 and N=5 reflexion conditions, trial-level DR hits minimum at trial 3 then increases — consistent pattern across conditions but not statistically significant (Wilson CIs overlap)
   - **Death dominance structural ceiling**: Multiple interventions (reflexion ~34%, 72B scaling ~34.5%, retry ~43%) all converge above deterministic 16.3%
   - **Wilson CI**: Statistical confidence intervals used in the unified metrics script
   - **Servers**: westb-13313 (retry data, 2× RTX 5090 32GB), westd-18266 (reflexion data, 4× RTX PRO 6000 96GB, cuda:0/1/2 allocated)
   - **Model**: Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (vLLM serving, temperature=0.3, seed=42)
   - **Cross-server data source**: N=1/N=2 reflexion from westb-13313, N=3/4/5 from westd-18266 — trial 1 DR differs by ~1pp between servers
   - **Coverage matrix**: C1: 83.3%, C1+C2: 100%, C2: 22.2% (informative negatives), Overall: 55.6%

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/analysis/unified_dose_response_data.json`** (westd-18266)
     - Reflexion unified metrics for N=3/N=4/N=5 with Wilson CIs
     - Key data at 188 eps (pre-completion): N=3 final=34.0% (68/200), N=4 final=36.5% (73/200), N=5 final=36.17% (68/188)
     - Cross-validated against condition_summary.json — matches exactly

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/analysis/unified_dose_response_data_retry.json`** (westb-13313)
     - Retry N=5 unified metrics: Trial 1: 48.0% (96/200), Final: 43.5% (87/200)
     - Conditional per-trial rates flat at ~47-50%
     - Registry's death_rate_n1=51.2% doesn't match trial 1's 48.0% — likely from different experiment source

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/reflexion_dose_response/reflexion_n3/condition_summary.json`** (westd)
     - 200 eps, 68 deaths, DR=34.0%, 10 tasks × 20 eps — verified matches unified data
     - Per-task range: 20% (inclined-plane) to 50% (freeze)

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/reflexion_dose_response/reflexion_n4/condition_summary.json`** (westd)
     - 200 eps, 73 deaths, DR=36.5%, 10 tasks × 20 eps — verified matches unified data
     - Per-task changes from N=3 are bidirectional: find-animal improved -15pp, lifespan-l-then-s worsened +15pp

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/retry_dose_response/retry_n5/condition_summary.json`** (westd)
     - Found to be dry-run residue: only 10 episodes (1/task), 0 deaths, 0 successes — NOT real data

   - **Individual episode JSONs** (westd reflexion_n5/):
     - Correct structure: `task_name`, `variation_idx`, `mode`="reflexion", `trials[]` with `trial_idx`/`score`/`is_death`/`is_success`/`num_steps`/`time_s`/`actions`/`reflection_text`, `final_is_death`, `final_score`
     - Verified three outcome types: all-death, mixed (rescue→regression), all-alive

   - **ROADMAP.md**: Evidence triangle needs updating — reflexion listed as "~35%" (actual N=3=34.0%), retry as "~44.5%*" estimate
   - **paper_framing.md**: U-shape finding consistent with "death dominance is structural" narrative
   - **decisions.yaml**: D114 has data discrepancies (reflexion "27%" is fictional, retry metric mixing)
   - **report.md**: reflexion-dose-response (killed) hypothesis said "plateau ~27-30%" — actual is 34-36.5%

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **ssh_read_only blocks python open()**: Attempted `python3 -c` with `json.load(open(...))` — blocked. Used `grep "is_death"` on individual files instead.
   - **ssh_read_only blocks pipe character**: `grep "is_death\|\"score\""` blocked. Used separate `grep "is_death"` commands without pipe.
   - **ssh_read_only blocks redirect**: `2>/dev/null` blocked. Avoided redirects in subsequent commands.
   - **decisions.yaml too large**: 88K chars exceeded tool limits. Used `tail -c 8000` via Bash to read latest decisions (D105-D114).

5. Problem Solving:
   - **U-shape discovery**: Identified non-monotonic reflexion per-trial trajectory where trial 3 is minimum then DR increases at trials 4-5. This is explained by mixed-outcome episodes where temporary rescue at trial 3 regresses at later trials (confirmed by spot-checking episodes like find-animal_ep005 [T,T,T,F,T] and grow-fruit_ep012 [T,T,F,T,T]).
   - **D114 data discrepancy**: Identified that D114's "reflexion 46%→27%" uses a fictional 27% from an old hypothesis, and compares retry metrics on different denominators (220 all-task eps vs 200 death-prone eps).
   - **Registry metric inconsistency**: death_rate_n1=0.512 doesn't match unified data's 48.0% — likely from plan-012-full baseline rather than retry experiment's trial 1.
   - **Dry-run residue**: westd retry_n5/condition_summary.json has only 10 episodes with 0 deaths — identified as non-real data, flagged for awareness.
   - **Dose-response narrative**: Determined safe framing is "diminishing returns with possible degradation beyond N=3," NOT "more reflexion always helps" — supported by N=3 being the minimum (34.0%) before plateau/rebound.

6. All user messages:

   - "[系统通知 — cron:on_complete:reflexion-dose-response-n345]: 实验 reflexion-dose-response-n345 complete，请审查。" (cron notification that N=5 experiment is complete)

Need to: (1) verify final unified data with all 200 N=5 episodes, (2) cross-validate N=5 condition_summary.json, (3) check final per-trial and final_dr numbers, (4) post complete audit report to Director covering all findings.

   - **Flag evidence triangle update needed**: ROADMAP numbers need correction with actual unified data.
   - **Verify unified script rerun**: Agent should rerun unify_dose_response_metrics.py with complete N=5 data (200 eps instead of 188).

8. Current Work:
   I was monitoring the reflexion-dose-response-n345 N=5 experiment's final episodes (measure-melting-point-unknown-substance ep015→ep019) on westd-18266. Progress tracked from 195/200 through 197/200 before receiving the cron completion notification. The experiment is now confirmed complete at 200/200 episodes. I had already:
   - Posted an interim warning review about the U-shape pattern and data discrepancies
   - Cross-validated N=3 (34.0%) and N=4 (36.5%) against condition_summary.json
   - Verified data quality through episode spot-checks
   - Confirmed GPU compliance (cuda:0/1/2 within allocation)
   - Reviewed ROADMAP, coverage matrix, paper_framing, and decisions.yaml
   - Identified multiple data consistency issues (D114 fictional 27%, retry metric mixing, registry n1 discrepancy)

9. Optional Next Step:
   The cron notification says "实验 reflexion-dose-response-n345 complete，请审查。" I need to:
   1. Verify N=5 final data: check that condition_summary.json for reflexion_n5 now exists and shows 200/200 episodes

```text
Let me verify the spot-check counts:

**Freeze (N=5)**: 
- true: ep000, ep003, ep004, ep005, ep007, ep008, ep009, ep012, ep015, ep016, ep017, ep018 = 12 deaths
- false: ep001, ep002, ep006, ep010, ep011, ep013, ep014, ep019 = 8 alive
- Total deaths = 12/20 = 60% ✓ matches condition_summary


- **Chemistry-mix (N=5)**: 4 deaths out of 20 episodes (20%) ✓
- **Grow-fruit (N=5)**: 5 deaths out of 20 episodes (25%) ✓

Both spot checks confirm the data integrity. Now I'm compiling the comprehensive review of the N=5 dataset — 200 total episodes across 10 tasks, with 70 deaths overall (35% death rate) and 26 successes (13%). The per-task death rates range from 20% in chemistry-mix up to 60% in freeze, and all my spot checks align perfectly with the condition summary. I'm ready to examine the complete dose-response curve across all N values from 1 through 5 using the unified data.

Looking at the dose-response pattern, N=1 starts around 47%, then drops to 37-39% at N=2, reaches a minimum at N=3 with 34%, and plateaus around 35-36.5% for N=4 and N=5. I notice the unified_dose_response_data.json file is outdated — it still shows N=5 at 188 episodes with a 36.17% death rate, but the actual complete dataset has 200 episodes with 35.0%, so I need to regenerate that file.

Within the N=5 trials themselves, there's a U-shaped pattern: Trial 1 peaks at 47.34%, drops to a minimum of 31.38% at Trial 3, then rebounds to 36.17% by Trial 5 — a swing of about 4.8 percentage points. The retry data from westb-13313 shows a different behavior: Trial 1 is at 48% but the conditional per-trial rates stay flat around 47-51%, with the final death rate settling at 43.5%, suggesting the retry follows a flatter trajectory than the original trials.

Now I'm noticing several inconsistencies across the registry and datasets. The reflexion-dose-response experiment is still in ANALYZING status with no metrics finalized, while its parent experiment claims a plateau around 27-30% which doesn't match the actual observed range of 34-36.5%. There's also a mismatch between the retry-dose-response death rate for N=1 (0.512) and what the unified retry trial 1 shows (0.48). The D114 interim review flagged some problematic metric mixing, including a fictional 27% figure being used in reflexion comparisons. GPU allocation looks compliant with the spec, so I should verify whether the experiment is still running or has completed.

```text
The Director is asking me to evaluate the U-shape pattern in reflexion dose-response data and provide framing recommendations. Let me analyze this carefully.

First, let me compile the complete data I have:

**Reflexion Final DR (from condition_summary, all 200 eps):**
- N=1: ~47.5% (from westb-13313, unified data shows different values per condition)
- N=2: ~40.0% (from westb-13313)
- N=3: 34.0% (68/200) — from westd-18266 condition_summary


- N=4: 36.5% (73/200) — from westd-18266 condition_summary
- N=5: 35.0% (70/200) — from westd-18266 condition_summary

I'm noticing a discrepancy where the Director cited N=5 at ~36.2%, but the actual complete dataset shows 35.0% from 70/200 episodes. The 36.2% figure appears to come from older unified data that only had 188 episodes, so I should flag this difference. The Wilson confidence intervals from the unified data are also stale for N=5, so I need to recalculate those with the full 200-episode dataset.

Now I'm examining the per-trial breakdown for N=5 across the 188-episode sample, which shows Trial 3 hitting the lowest point at 31.38%, while Trial 1 spiked to 47.34%. With the complete data in hand, I'm ready to assess whether the rebound from N=3 to N=4/N=5 is statistically meaningful.

For the N=3 to N=4 comparison, I'm running a two-proportion z-test with the 2.5 percentage point increase (68 to 73 deaths out of 200), calculating the pooled standard error and z-statistic to determine if this shift is just noise or a genuine effect.

The p-value comes out to about 0.60, which is nowhere near statistical significance—this difference is easily explained by random variation. When I compare N=3 to N=5, the results are even more striking: the proportions are nearly identical (34% vs 35%), yielding a p-value around 0.83. The Wilson confidence intervals for these groups overlap substantially, confirming that N=3, N=4, and N=5 are essentially indistinguishable from each other.

Looking at the framing options, the safest approach is to say that Reflexion shows diminishing returns and plateaus around N=3 at roughly 34-36%, since none of the differences between N=3, N=4, and N=5 are statistically meaningful. But when I look at the actual trajectory across all N values—starting at 47.5% for N=1 and settling around 35% by N=5—there's a clearer story about how performance drops off.

I think the best framing combines honesty with the data: Reflexion reduces the death rate from about 47% down to a plateau of 34-36% at N≥3, with no meaningful gains beyond that point. This avoids overstating the U-shape pattern that the statistical tests don't support.

For the Director's point about death dominance, they're right that what ultimately matters is that floor around 34-36%, regardless of whether the path there is perfectly monotonic or has minor fluctuations.

On whether to run more diagnostics, the overlapping confidence intervals and the fact that N=5 complete data (35.0%) matches N=3 (34.0%) pretty closely suggest it's not worth the GPU time. Those resources would be better allocated elsewhere. Though I should flag that the Director cited N=5≈36.2%, but the actual complete data shows 35.0%—a meaningful difference that shifts the interpretation slightly.

This is the strongest cross-scale evidence for death dominance and directly\\n    addresses R15-W3 (SF4). R15 全部 action items (MF1-3, SF1-5, references) dispatched\\n    to Paper Agent in one comprehensive patch.'\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: backbone-scaling-70b\\n    role: primary\\n    result: 72B DAF=90.9%, label fraction=84.3%\\n  - exp_id: backbone-scaling-70b-parallel\\n    role: supplement\\n    result: 3 tasks data source\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D107\\n- id: D109\\n  date: 2026-05-18 04:08 UTC\\n  summary: Go Crafter permadeath pilot (50 eps) to address C3 env diversity gap, targeting\\n    Conference from Findings 3.0/5\\n  rationale: 'R22=3.0/5, C3 (lack of permadeath env replication) is the key Findings→Conference\\n    differentiator. Agent feasibility study shows Crafter viable: LLM death rate 60-80%\\n    vs heuristic 20-40%, BALROG text wrapper available, $10-15 cost. Reviewer concerned\\n    about \\\"deterministic modality\\\" definition but heuristic agent with full state\\n    observation is consistent with paper''s core insight. User directive: \\\"冲 Conference\\\".\\n    Go/no-go checkpoints: Day 2 demo, Day 3 asymmetry ≥15pp.'\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: retry-dose-response\\n    role: parallel\\n    result: running N=5\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D108\\n  - D107\\n- id: D110\\n  date: 2026-05-18 06:01 UTC\\n  summary: Death taxonomy reveals 100% deaths from single action-space grounding failure\\n    (focus on misuse); writing into paper as Significance booster\\n  rationale: P4 failure mode taxonomy found that ALL 73 internal-strategy deaths are\\n    caused by LLM misusing `focus on` (answer-submission action) as exploration. 69.9%\\n    have task awareness but lack grounding. Deterministic agent survives 91.8% by\\n    never using focus on. This transforms \\\"death dominates\\\" from descriptive to mechanistic,\\n    significantly boosting Significance dimension (2.5→3.0 target).\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: plan-012-full\\n    role: data source\\n    result: 73 death episodes analyzed\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D109\\n  - D107\\n- id: D111\\n  date: 2026-05-18 06:05 UTC\\n  summary: 'Kill Crafter pilot: heuristic agent 100% death rate after 10 versions,\\n    no death rate asymmetry possible'\\n  rationale: 'Go/no-go checkpoint failed: heuristic agent death rate = 100% (same\\n    as LLM), asymmetry = 0pp (threshold was ≥15pp). **Agent 必须在 westd-18266 重跑 unify_dose_response_metrics.py 更新此文件**\n\n2. **[severity: warning] D114 仍保留虚构的 \"reflexion 陡降 46%→27%\" 表述**\n   - 证据：D114 rationale 原文 \"与 reflexion 形成鲜明对比（retry 平坦 ~42% vs reflexion 陡降 46%→27%）\"\n   - 问题：27% 从未在任何实验中观测到。实际 reflexion 最低点是 N=3=34.0%\n   - D115 已用正确数字，但 D114 作为历史记录仍会误导后续读者\n   - 建议：在 D114 中追加 errata 注释标注 27% 为旧假设非实测值\n\n3. **[severity: warning] 分析报告使用的 retry N=1 DR=51.2% 与 unified retry 数据不一致**\n   - 证据：报告表格第 1 行 \"Retry DR = 51.2%\"，来源是 registry retry-dose-response.metrics.death_rate_n1\n   - 但 unified_dose_response_data_retry.json 中 trial 1 DR=48.0% (96/200)\n   - 51.2% 可能源自 plan-012-full baseline（220 eps, 含非 death-prone task find-non-living-thing）\n   - 如果 reflexion N=1 (47.5%) 基于 200 death-prone eps，而 retry N=1 (51.2%) 基于 220 all-task eps，这是**不公平对比（不同分母）**\n   - 建议：统一使用 200 death-prone eps 口径，retry N=1 应为 48.0%\n\n4. **[severity: info] 报告缺少 per-trial trajectory 分析（U-shape 数据）**\n   - D115 提到 trial-3 最低的模式，但分析报告只覆盖 final_dr per N condition\n   - 论文如果要提及 U-shape 作为 descriptive observation，需要 per-trial 数据支撑\n   - unified data 有此数据（但目前 N=5 是旧的），建议在更新 unified data 后补充 per-trial 分析到报告\n\n5. **[severity: info] report.md 中 reflexion-dose-response（killed 的父实验）仍显示 \"plateau 在 ~27-30%\"**\n   - 这是旧假设，从未被实验验证。不影响当前实验但可能误导未来阅读者\n\n### GPU 合规\n- [info] 实验已完成，westd-18266 四卡全部空闲（0% util, 0 MiB）。实验运行期间使用 cuda:0/1/2，在白名单内。✓",
  "project": "simgate-modular-routing"
}
